> In partnership with [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/75861363-f128-4507-bc94-b184f8f8d988?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) 

##### Lululemon: Headlines say “nosedive,” and the [data](https://substack.com/redirect/2e5a4bf0-cc85-46f1-974e-96941abf51fd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) can’t entirely refute it.

About a week ago, the founder of Lululemon, Chip Wilson, did something very unusual: he placed [an ad in the Wall Street Journal](https://substack.com/redirect/dd11b52c-8a54-4296-b902-ff69461047ce?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) titled “Lululemon: In a nosedive”. Wilson exited the company in 2015, and claims that since then the company has faltered, citing a few reasons: loss of creative talent, internal culture erosion, and the “loss of cool”.

In the past 6 months, [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/75861363-f128-4507-bc94-b184f8f8d988?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) has found that Lululemon has had no statistically significant change in any of its brand health metrics - not exactly a “nosedive” on the brand front, but not growth, either. On an overall Awareness basis, Lululemon is still dominant, only falling behind other big brands like Nike, Adidas and Puma – but converting slower than the likes of Nike and Adidas from Awareness down to Consideration.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!93Uz!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F0fff55f1-2d3c-4c03-aa00-ae2e169c4349_2336x1526.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/2e5a4bf0-cc85-46f1-974e-96941abf51fd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)In terms of consumer perception on whether Lululemon really has lost its innovative edge, as Wilson claims? Well, [Tracksuit data](https://substack.com/redirect/2e5a4bf0-cc85-46f1-974e-96941abf51fd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) shows there might be some truth to that, with the brand sitting behind the competitive average on the statement, “Is innovative” (only 22% agree).

While we’ve got you here, [Miranda from Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/2e5a4bf0-cc85-46f1-974e-96941abf51fd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) has put together [a quick video](https://substack.com/redirect/2e5a4bf0-cc85-46f1-974e-96941abf51fd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) of how you can use the dashboard to make a big, bold campaign bet, and prove it worked.

> ⚰️ Report Break: Nearly 8 in 10 Americans say politicians should avoid heated or aggressive speech. A far smaller share (20%) says that officials should be able to use heated language without worrying about how some people may act, according to a [Pew Research Center](https://substack.com/redirect/988bac67-afb6-4cf6-9be8-335100eba840?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) survey.

> [Black Friday](https://substack.com/redirect/89a2412e-d560-4d96-b704-7ac10494b9c7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): Google Shopping Ads continue to run after items go out of stock, triggering cost-per-click charges with little to no chance of conversion. According to a ShoppingIQ study of 500 global retailers, 97% continued to [pay for clicks](https://substack.com/redirect/89a2412e-d560-4d96-b704-7ac10494b9c7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on unavailable products, often for 24 to 48 hours.

> 🥀 Report Break: 60% of marketer respondents said [advertising placed in news](https://substack.com/redirect/57dd418b-c090-41cb-8262-4fbebb957b2c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) environments performs better than campaign baselines, and 27% said it performs on par with baselines. Just 11% of respondents found that news inventory performed below [average compared](https://substack.com/redirect/9825f7b3-ce8f-4641-b0ac-517a3eed353f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with other media.

> A tiny portion of marketers – just 1% – said they consider all types of news content to be unsuitable for advertising.

* OpenAI dumps [Mixpanel](https://substack.com/redirect/e2b35948-2963-43aa-af13-0ca0ffd37b29?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) after analytics breach hits API users.
	+ OpenAI is [just](https://substack.com/redirect/1537b65e-e149-4401-8be4-42e6da6f0719?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) [$200 billion away](https://substack.com/redirect/1537b65e-e149-4401-8be4-42e6da6f0719?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from still losing money, HSBC says.
	+ OpenAI [denies allegations](https://substack.com/redirect/7f444529-4554-435e-9fc9-ef33e78aa50a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) that ChatGPT is to blame for a teenager’s suicide.
* [UK government](https://substack.com/redirect/1dc03610-f1e5-474b-8a4e-8ba95e8394e6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) will buy tech to boost AI sector in $130M growth push.
* Tech companies tap [debt markets](https://substack.com/redirect/121bc734-fc48-491c-9ad8-6fb6c7d3449f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to fund AI and cloud expansion.
* New data reveals [what people actually do](https://substack.com/redirect/93d9cebc-9cbb-4ce5-8c77-f82fcea903c9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with ChatGPT – and it’s not just generating text.

---

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!0cUN!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F79af7349-0432-4bf9-83ad-f76d0fd2a362_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/27f4b677-4596-47a8-b0fa-9efe85f3aa1e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Microsoft faces [uphill climb](https://substack.com/redirect/1a42394e-eec6-449b-99fc-490937cde2d6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to turn enterprise dominance into widespread AI chatbot adoption.
* LinkedIn outlines [seven promotion tactics](https://substack.com/redirect/2be9e4c7-a852-4a19-9073-765939ab4186?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for newsletter subscriber growth.
* Microsoft’s head of AI infrastructure [departs](https://substack.com/redirect/c3ab361c-d12c-4b23-bfe3-7f51b30a3c73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* UNICEPTA [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/a3ed9d82-32bc-427e-b53d-da82fc9eedb6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) integration with Microsoft 365 Copilot to simplify reputation intelligence.
* Microsoft [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/aa63d463-fb7d-4d03-8f29-712fcb882db9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) new AI Shopping tools for Edge Browser.
* An AI startup’s viral LinkedIn story and the ‘[fake it till you make it](https://substack.com/redirect/0c0fa3ed-3ba1-4327-b21d-9319dd45cad1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ approach.
* Microsoft’s AI chatbot Copilot leaves WhatsApp on [January 15](https://substack.com/redirect/22014abb-c787-478b-b0d2-d30ba01e4239?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Microsoft might be [raising Xbox prices](https://substack.com/redirect/70d41626-4bf6-43d6-8fb5-85c8b151ae5e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for a third time.
* Microsoft unveils [Fara-7B](https://substack.com/redirect/d536f83e-c38e-4c07-9849-6b41d9a4b5a9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), a compact model for running AI-driven computer control locally.

---

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!WMjD!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F605790ce-ce9b-44a1-a77e-bc12503caa70_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/5597c701-f44e-4f45-8cf1-bd8280ddf766?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* [OOH ad revenue](https://substack.com/redirect/22309da0-7a44-4a84-965f-7fdb6534ca4e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) growth outstrips DOOH in Q3.
* Daily Mail’s new agency expands [social media publishing team](https://substack.com/redirect/3acc4892-5c6a-4e64-9637-3fca12c9bc7c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with senior hires.
* [Winners and losers](https://substack.com/redirect/4ec6d248-602e-4019-9ecb-7ad78345532c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) of Black Friday 2025.
* Channel 4, ITV and Sky commercial chiefs ask [advertisers](https://substack.com/redirect/21c4aa3d-33cb-422a-8ab3-9b0fa23e6ba1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to ‘turn down the toxic.’
* Blue Ant Media to buy [Thunderbird Entertainment](https://substack.com/redirect/00d87c0b-39ea-4208-8b77-e437554f94d7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in $63 million deal.
* [WPP](https://substack.com/redirect/966abdfd-eb3f-443a-ae98-a21979364337?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) announces official opening of its third London campus.
* OUTFRONT expands [experiential marketing](https://substack.com/redirect/ee20c38d-7eb6-48ed-8842-5b045e8f33a2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) focus ahead of major 2025-26 sports events and other cultural tentpoles.
* Dedicated interactive and localized ad formats are the [new focus](https://substack.com/redirect/243cf365-32d4-4792-bc84-548adb336133?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in CTV arms race.
* OnAudience [integrates](https://substack.com/redirect/62ee0d9c-14e2-4239-8e92-9c3fe0c49f39?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with Amazon Ads to boost AI-driven audience targeting.
* IAB Europe releases [new study](https://substack.com/redirect/2a74a7f4-d5ce-44a1-890e-b1f16b8cc42d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on the adoption of addressability and measurement solutions across Europe.
* Little Dot Studios [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/47d92cc9-3f66-45d6-be6b-afff537a090e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) ChIP platform;
* Teads partners with [XPLN.AI](https://substack.com/redirect/bd3f4bcd-43dd-49ec-ae7e-20e29f232f7e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to advance attention measurement.
* Swiss public broadcaster SRG to cut [900 jobs](https://substack.com/redirect/5c500d80-dd0e-467f-a4a0-51cca7485e97?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Broadcast giant [Sinclair](https://substack.com/redirect/4c375c78-b707-4df6-af83-84badb0f096d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) makes bid to buy out EW Scripps for $7 per share.
* MFE’s [European Project](https://substack.com/redirect/d7972323-886b-4c30-a5d3-90b776c56d03?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) takes another step forward with Impresa deal.

> 🧶 Report Break: 21% of UK video-on-demand (SVOD) subscribers [admit to subscription cycling](https://substack.com/redirect/e2cac6dd-ff83-4692-b06f-13020ffa9e2e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) — the highest ever recorded, according to the latest ScreenThink report.

> The study also found that an additional 42% of Brits are “open” to cycling, implying a growing normalisation of churn.

<h1 dir="auto" style="direction: auto;text-align: start;unicode-bidi: isolate;color: rgb(54,55,55);font-family: 'SF Pro Display', -apple-system, system-ui, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Inter', 'Segoe UI', Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif, 'Apple Color Emoji', 'Segoe UI Emoji', 'Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: 900;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 0;line-height: 36px;font-size: 32px;"><a href="https://substack.com/app-link/post?publication\_id=712314&amp;post\_id=180267404&amp;utm\_source=post-email-title&amp;utm\_campaign=email-post-title&amp;isFreemail=true&amp;r=6q6egl&amp;token=eyJ1c2VyX2lkIjo0MDY3NjU3NDksInBvc3RfaWQiOjE4MDI2NzQwNCwiaWF0IjoxNzY0NTQzMjg3LCJleHAiOjE3NjcxMzUyODcsImlzcyI6InB1Yi03MTIzMTQiLCJzdWIiOiJwb3N0LXJlYWN0aW9uIn0.I3-yRBgmc-ua2osy6086Jjhm956mCBzYCe4xCCYJ834" style="color: rgb(54,55,55);text-decoration: none;">This Week in Marketing: Ads in ChatGPT, YouTube Algorithm, AI Music, U.S. Senators, and Nvidia vs. Google</a></h1>
<h3 dir="auto" style="direction: auto;text-align: start;unicode-bidi: isolate;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: normal;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 4px 0 0;color: #777777;line-height: 24px;font-size: 18px;margin-top: 12px;">What was that? Senators question Meta and Trump, ads in ChatGPT soon-ish, Google chips vs. Nvidia, music labels are now friends with AI, and YouTube is trying a new algorithm.</h3>
<table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" role="presentation" style="margin: 1em 0;height: 20px;align-items: center;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>

<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;"><span>This newsletter is supported by our friends at </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ce2872e3-547a-40e7-8a08-e33631463844?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span>, the affordable, always-on brand tracking dashboard. </span></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Before you go further, Attention is like money–you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you'll get dividends for years.</p></blockquote>

<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Senator wants Trump to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4323dea2-82b8-4e8e-b5ac-63e247d71561?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">answer questions</a><span> on TikTok divestiture plan.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/869d953d-47b1-4473-b59d-ace9ee1421ee?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Code</a><span> suggests that </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a5df4e67-82ce-4834-9a16-c156b8061a9e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">OpenAI</a><span> may be close to introducing </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5585a279-8667-43c8-bf34-d2ad30c0cb5d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ads for ChatGPT</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta is in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a13b2305-9a11-4148-a9f2-6a3199166fb3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">talks</a><span> to use </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4b934d72-7e34-4467-944b-48fed4d12d86?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Google’s chips</a><span> in challenge to Nvidia.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>U.S. consumers </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ecdf9b5b-b041-4f5c-8b9c-6208946531ce?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">spent</a><span> a record </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/17ff695f-7478-4643-86f4-d6d85613a02c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$11.8 billion</a><span> online during Black Friday sales.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>X’s messy </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e0dd6385-9753-41c2-b127-f881ef9d9014?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">About This Account rollout</a><span> has caused utter chaos.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ba5778a3-b281-43d8-aee6-4701f3f06c04?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">More AI</a><span>: Will YouTube’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1de4316a-c4a8-46c1-af3e-995d625d0105?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new promptable feed</a><span> replace the recommendation algorithm?</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Why </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/48b80aec-961e-4cc8-a8fe-4e07f9e076b8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Pinterest’s CEO</a><span> is betting the company’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/742435aa-660d-4479-bd58-3aa930c4175d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">future on search</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>US senators call for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/271a0640-de58-41c8-b5d6-a7dd8b2b66d4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">investigation of scam ads</a><span> on Facebook and Instagram.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/df566382-b558-4d93-bc73-5a8b103533aa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Spotify</a><span> acquires </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d34a81ee-5b4a-4ff5-a95f-3329bc83e1ab?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">WhoSampled</a><span>, announces expanded Song Credits.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Why people are posting fake tales about being </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/164d8c2f-fba9-40f1-9c17-0767d9de2dd7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ghosted by brands</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>OpenAI partners amass </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d2c26dae-1bf5-4a61-b1d5-5d80ffd1179a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$100 billion debt pile</a><span> to fund its ambitions.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3f4e1b9d-f11f-4fa2-b603-86f97110b0e7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Insurers retreat</a><span> from AI cover as risk of multibillion-dollar claims mounts.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Warner Bros Discovery seeks </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/094c2ff8-dacc-4eb2-9893-bdb78d9ad165?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">improved bids</a><span> by December 1, sources say.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Omnicom closes </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c34d027e-f448-4902-96ff-0fb3fa70e6c5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$13 billion deal</a><span> for IPG, creating advertising and communications giant.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/410faac2-b057-4fa9-b741-c2d0a5afd11e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI Slop recipes</a><span> are taking over the internet — and Thanksgiving dinner.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/651b23ee-8a48-459d-85dc-c8bb3af3c81a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Campbell’s</a><span> says executive who </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ea6e28ec-f3b9-41aa-abba-d0a09b6ee6ac?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">disparaged</a><span> its food has left the company.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Rage-bait? Internet users stunned as </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2482346d-3088-40b4-938a-c848770affa0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Suno user complains</a><span> about </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/44f1c2fd-1435-4df0-997b-9879c9546a2d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">running out of prompt ideas</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Warner Music </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ee2b763f-87a8-4fed-a0f0-fb29c21f8cc5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">signs deal with AI song generator Suno</a><span> after settling lawsuit.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Suno creates a </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/90db8ca8-fcd2-44b2-8c12-b972abe0942a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Spotify catalog’s worth of AI music</a><span> every two weeks.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>In partnership with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/75861363-f128-4507-bc94-b184f8f8d988?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span> </span></p></blockquote>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">
<span>Lululemon: Headlines say “nosedive,” and the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2e5a4bf0-cc85-46f1-974e-96941abf51fd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: rgb(54,55,55);text-decoration: underline;">data</a><span> can’t entirely refute it.</span>
</h3>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>About a week ago, the founder of Lululemon, Chip Wilson, did something very unusual: he placed </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dd11b52c-8a54-4296-b902-ff69461047ce?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">an ad in the Wall Street Journal</a><span> titled “Lululemon: In a nosedive”. Wilson exited the company in 2015, and claims that since then the company has faltered, citing a few reasons: loss of creative talent, internal culture erosion, and the “loss of cool”.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>In the past 6 months, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/75861363-f128-4507-bc94-b184f8f8d988?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span> has found that Lululemon has had no statistically significant change in any of its brand health metrics - not exactly a “nosedive” on the brand front, but not growth, either. On an overall Awareness basis, Lululemon is still dominant, only falling behind other big brands like Nike, Adidas and Puma – but converting slower than the likes of Nike and Adidas from Awareness down to Consideration.</span></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="692"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2e5a4bf0-cc85-46f1-974e-96941abf51fd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/0fff55f1-2d3c-4c03-aa00-ae2e169c4349\_2336x1526.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":951,"width":1456,"resizeWidth":692,"bytes":373624,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":"https://hubs.li/Q03Wcr540","belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/180267404?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F0fff55f1-2d3c-4c03-aa00-ae2e169c4349\_2336x1526.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="359.2376373626374" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!93Uz!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F0fff55f1-2d3c-4c03-aa00-ae2e169c4349\_2336x1526.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>In terms of consumer perception on whether Lululemon really has lost its innovative edge, as Wilson claims? Well, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2e5a4bf0-cc85-46f1-974e-96941abf51fd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit data</a><span> shows there might be some truth to that, with the brand sitting behind the competitive average on the statement, “Is innovative” (only 22% agree).</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>While we’ve got you here, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2e5a4bf0-cc85-46f1-974e-96941abf51fd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Miranda from Tracksuit</a><span> has put together </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2e5a4bf0-cc85-46f1-974e-96941abf51fd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a quick video</a><span> of how you can use the dashboard to make a big, bold campaign bet, and prove it worked. </span></p>
<p data-attrs='{"url":"https://hubs.li/Q03Wcr540","text":"See Tracksuit in action!","action":null,"class":null}' data-component-name="ButtonCreateButton" style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2e5a4bf0-cc85-46f1-974e-96941abf51fd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="font-family: system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';display: inline-block;box-sizing: border-box;cursor: pointer;border: none;border-radius: 8px;font-size: 14px;line-height: 20px;font-weight: 600;text-align: center;margin: 0;opacity: 1;outline: none;white-space: nowrap;color: #ffffff !important;text-decoration: none !important;background-color: #e85c57;padding: 12px 20px;height: auto;"><span style="color: #ffffff;text-decoration: none;">See Tracksuit in action!</span></a></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Facebook scam ads prompt </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fed14875-8867-45c1-9048-44225a17211a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">criminal complaint</a><span> against Zuckerberg.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/74dfc5b1-f4d6-44b6-90d8-49457c3f7725?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">OpenAI</a><span> and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7be33f37-b6b6-4473-b350-20bca26e7a78?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Perplexity</a><span> are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/99be1186-1c0e-4b55-bbbd-ce7deb33712d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">launching</a><span> AI shopping assistants, but competing startups aren’t sweating it.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>The American Dream sold to Gen Z feels further from </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c7a1f22b-ecd3-4633-92ac-8783b5c61b94?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">reality</a><span> than ever, according to a new study from </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0432396f-a835-4069-a572-fcf9fa1de13b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Oxford Economics</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Fewer one night stands, more AI lovers: the data behind </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d894f259-66a2-4b0e-a4c2-624291108787?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">generation Z’s sex lives</a><span>.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Deloitte allegedly cited </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cbc7bf35-3601-4af5-b159-4a3a6beddd22?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI-generated research</a><span> in a million-dollar report for a Canadian provincial government.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>“Wicked: For Good” </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9863e444-74b7-4e23-bdd8-ac039b3591dd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">defies gravity</a><span> at box office, despite poor critical reception.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Microsoft ditches publishing ‘traditional’ </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/66c16454-6785-4eee-ad6f-931b9ccce04b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">diversity and inclusion report</a><span> this year.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>How Joe Preston got </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/289fe10c-8c1f-40b6-92d0-7131a96b3aaf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">New Balance</a><span> back in the race.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Character.AI launches </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2733f420-b951-41ca-a655-265dc56e7fc6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Stories for teens</a><span> after banning them from chats.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Teens are saying </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5e8b2fbf-0955-4e03-9b66-d7bd055be1b2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">tearful goodbyes</a><span> to their AI companions.</span></p></li></ul>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>New NBA and MLB TV deals will drive </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/87976a09-0998-4ac9-8702-dca6b4e212a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">global sports-media spend </a><span>to $78 Billion in five years.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Construction of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/56ece062-0dd6-4665-b1df-6554c2d3f808?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Meta data center</a><span> causing ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1389fab0-a9b5-482d-b999-16581317da17?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">dangerous and reckless</a><span>’ road conditions.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>AI can already do the work of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/35f97f60-9834-4574-bb0b-81644c87249d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">12% of America’s workforce</a><span>, MIT researchers find.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b9eb0b90-f3aa-40ca-8668-e577c446be82?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">fate of Google’s adtech monopoly</a><span> is now in a judge’s hands.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>No, your favourite influencer hasn’t got </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/58f8a75a-8b6b-4243-9367-6a5c50ebf114?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a dozen dachshund dogs</a><span>. It’s just AI.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>EU to examine if </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/03b73f78-16d0-40f1-bd1d-a8d67d52c208?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Apple Ads and Maps</a><span> subject to tough rules, Apple says no.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>CISA warns </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ff30a0ab-078a-4f82-8536-d83c824b16e2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">spyware crews</a><span> are breaking into Signal and WhatsApp accounts.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d66a09e8-9cca-4d06-a749-ae5c0e68f24e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Slop Evader</a><span>’ lets you surf the web like it’s 2022.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>UK’s S4 Capital, M&amp;C Saatchi warn on profits in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b94dfe4e-1bad-4e5d-9848-2acc34d57912?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">tough ad market</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>WPP set to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1cd0d600-4964-4558-b68e-32e5fa5d20a8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">drop out of FTSE 100 Index</a><span> after 27-Year stint.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/292de9a9-ce90-4eec-82ac-1fbdad032eb7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Food52</a><span> explores sale after years of revenue decline.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Americans are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7b00cd53-d1c8-4b4c-b6ca-35d9bca38a3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">microdosing obesity drugs</a><span>, driven by ‘Thin Is In’ marketing blitz.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/781457e9-f93d-4b0e-9972-9bc11608454f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span> and Small World’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f2d3dcfd-a184-48fd-b2d2-31d0d35e0df6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">‘Entertain or Die’ report</a><span> reveals how brands are building in-house Hollywoods, worlds consumers escape into, filled with brand characters, lore, and social content that keeps them talking.\*\*</span></p></blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>TJ Maxx, Homegoods, Marshalls soar as </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/18d164e8-bec1-47fe-bcba-0d91a929425a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">shoppers</a><span> pull back.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b18e16dc-6864-4ec0-aaf5-f910b3f5c6c9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Debenhams</a><span> debuts </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4aae4ad2-a77c-430d-9ed4-7c5fd03a3df1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">self-service retail media network</a><span> for 15,000 marketplace brands. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>U.S. retail sales </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b1cb8922-9a7b-4f73-a672-75eb48ff4545?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">rose modestly</a><span> in September, as consumers pulled back spending after months of splurging.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6dba59ac-fcf0-40f3-9f8b-b4ac2edd09de?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">America’s polarization</a><span> has become the world’s ultimate side hustle.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Why </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/df567cd6-9fde-4912-ad8c-17ed0b085c5f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">college students</a><span> prefer TikTok over newspapers. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f4c62608-05df-4007-a984-dc36519cba51?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Alibaba</a><span> launches</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bb280dae-34e0-4689-a62f-d649dbaf514d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> $537 Quark AI glasses</a><span> to challenge wearables from Meta, Xiaomi, Rokid.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Americans are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9dec8195-ec1f-4095-802c-9e26495c721a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">holding onto devices longer than ever</a><span> and it’s costing the economy.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Facebook takes on Reddit with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/035a18c7-96f0-4f5e-a57f-f72fe03c2f1d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">launch of nicknames</a><span> for Facebook Groups.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta’s ad platform is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/447bfc24-4e93-4fb5-8f1c-68a3a8e9040c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">going haywire</a><span> in time for the holidays (again).</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>AI meets </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2ef9c49f-e27d-4294-af50-a0c5952b4665?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">aggressive accounting</a><span> at Meta’s gigantic new data center.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta integrates more </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/12642e8a-ec41-4a07-bf5d-4a20994f8e68?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI-powered options</a><span> into its ad platform.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a0c1ddb5-addd-4213-92df-2d6584bbe79e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">fires back at allegations</a><span> that it silenced research linking Facebook to depression, anxiety, and loneliness.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta slams EU’s ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2df6b525-c87f-4dcb-b266-447f606aaea2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">aberrant</a><span>’ antitrust demands for data on Facebook.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>ChatGPT and Copilot are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b4e5add4-096b-464b-9903-f3a309127043?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">being booted out of</a><span> WhatsApp.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ae6a8b17-5b4a-4a74-b15d-a148eedf1f9a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Italy antitrust watchdog</a><span> may curb Meta as WhatsApp AI probe widens.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta’s new </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c07a0d70-a278-4aed-9896-ca637d6d7c91?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">wearables trade-in program</a><span> gives you Ray-Ban and Oakley discounts.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Google changes Gemini 3 Pro free </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/09667e70-9936-42d1-be80-ab16951c0743?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">access limits</a><span> due to ‘high demand’.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Gemini is set to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ace683e7-07f1-4348-88e7-ecad12a3f507?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">replace Google Assistant</a><span> on Android Auto soon/</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Adthena becomes first to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2ad7ffd9-5333-4aea-b3d2-6e8471151fd5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">detect ads in Google AI Overview</a><span> search results.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google Business Profiles adds </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1078558d-2eb4-4e68-9fae-5311b04372d8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">scheduling and multi-location publishing</a><span> to Google Posts.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google Ads advisor </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1783a274-ee74-4ba0-b4d7-2da9f0a652ee?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">may take context</a><span> from other MCC client chats.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google’s Mueller says sites in a ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ab1b1963-db76-49a9-9bc5-9cb119d5a282?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">bad state</a><span>’ may need to start over.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google Ads overview tab now supports </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/62b2850f-148e-4309-9cbe-9912cca4a99f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">custom views</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google Performance Max gets </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ad2a6cf0-0167-4ff4-b377-5414f575ef44?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">direct video uploads</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google drops </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e9ebf43a-6af8-430e-aeaa-850f09dd7f0b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">EU antitrust complaint</a><span> against Microsoft amid cloud probe.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/89a2412e-d560-4d96-b704-7ac10494b9c7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Black Friday</a><span>: Google Shopping Ads continue to run after items go out of stock, triggering cost-per-click charges with little to no chance of conversion. According to a ShoppingIQ study of 500 global retailers, 97% continued to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/89a2412e-d560-4d96-b704-7ac10494b9c7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">pay for clicks</a><span> on unavailable products, often for 24 to 48 hours.</span></p></blockquote>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Small changes to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f6534be0-cca8-45d1-bd33-1a4f46e2e177?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">‘for you’ feed on X</a><span> can rapidly increase political polarisation.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Nvidia’s strengths are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5904b079-3657-49b6-b8b0-ab4d64993cf0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">not in its X posts</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>DOGE “cut muscle, not fat”; </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/26a977f1-4e29-420b-9a2e-326d94719535?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">26K experts rehired</a><span> after brutal cuts.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Dutch public broadcaster NOS </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e25d9ee4-d966-4725-b2e8-f1189a5549cd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">quits</a><span> X over disinformation.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Musk’s xAI to close </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0a6aa8ee-91f3-4fa7-9873-b94267a8aaeb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$15 billion funding round</a><span> in December: sources.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f40273ba-dff1-453d-8eae-dbb1fe315d45?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Grokipedia</a><span> now has an edit history.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Musk’s xAI to build small solar farm adjacent to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/82d153b0-fd9f-4202-ab77-da127c37e74e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Colossus data center</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>X has brought back the old “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2ee111ba-26ab-4da0-ba31-8719c8b1c115?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Manipulated media</a><span>” tag?</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>X is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c7c18406-efd3-4622-9c13-6b72d3b76657?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">working</a><span> on something called “Creator Studio.”</span></p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🥀 Report Break: 60% of marketer respondents said </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/57dd418b-c090-41cb-8262-4fbebb957b2c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">advertising placed in news</a><span> environments performs better than campaign baselines, and 27% said it performs on par with baselines. Just 11% of respondents found that news inventory performed below </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9825f7b3-ce8f-4641-b0ac-517a3eed353f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">average compared</a><span> with other media.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">A tiny portion of marketers – just 1% – said they consider all types of news content to be unsuitable for advertising.</p>
</blockquote>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e871c975-90b7-4dc2-8e71-339946f21429?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Plex’s crackdown</a><span> on free remote streaming access starts this week.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Gamified app Newsreel </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c182cdf3-f1a6-4213-807f-6ea5e8e3c99e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">secures</a><span> $100k funding boost.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>News publishers who are beating </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5a79db40-3f0a-4b72-b987-27757f12e7af?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">social media networks</a><span> at their own game.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>NSA contractor groomed teenage girls on </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/921a14c7-5bbf-4164-97dd-e4b9142e3bf3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Reddit</a><span>, DOJ alleges.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>An </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/77a5c527-b095-42f7-b120-90e601ab1709?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">algorithm-free feed</a><span> for $1,999/month.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Beehiiv’s CEO</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3b067a68-0c84-4d6b-b3ff-45703a041cc3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> isn’t worried</a><span> about newsletter saturation.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Apple News just lost </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/674fa46f-d149-463f-8d01-82af96108f42?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a major media partner</a><span> after a decade.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ff5e4c99-d91a-4a93-9645-88b97ced803a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Singapore</a><span> orders Apple, Google to prevent government spoofing on messaging platforms.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Apple </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/53c91764-0c03-4c37-9fb5-6b03dfcb7ed5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">challenges</a><span> India antitrust rule that could trigger $38b fine.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Epic CEO Tim Sweeney says </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9e6a0865-95f3-44dd-a7e6-64ea8238ab03?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Steam</a><span> should drop its ‘Made with AI’ tags.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Substack </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2c1313db-df67-4027-b0a0-bd42369e280b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">finally</a><span> did it. They </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b033d763-5642-4706-9484-4ac78f516691?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">added</a><span> a film and television category.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bf322b56-a1ab-474a-b2f9-5f86521bcaea?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Consumer Reports</a><span> revives a 1940s-era newsletter for cash-strapped Americans on Substack.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Roblox is a problem — but it’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8299956b-6395-4052-bd8f-cf590bfbb5aa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a symptom</a><span> of something worse.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>UFC to make </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7173e1f6-4333-4d8c-9e85-7eea289e9fae?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Paramount+</a><span> debut on Jan. 24.</span></p></li>
</ul>
